;*******************************************************************************************************************************
;** Coverage: the TSV row and the clipboard text of X. stackregister_csv_out writes each register through                     **
;** copyRegisterToClipboardString2, utf8ToString and stringToASCII; TSX runs the same three calls on X and checks the text. The **
;** row is the stored value with trailing zeros dropped, a radix added to an integer, then the angle unit: the display format, **
;** DRM and RM do not reach it, so each value expects the same text under every setting. FIX 2, SCI 3, ENG 2, SIG 3 with TRL0 **
;** set, SIG 3 with TRL0 clear, ALL 3, UNIT 2; DRM 0 to 6, each with a different RM; the six points of drm_round_cov after    **
;** digit 3 and 11, at exponents -12, 0 and 20; each positive and negative. Also a complex, long integers, and angles in DEG, **
;** D.MS, RAD, GRAD and MULTPI. Expected: Python decimal from the rules in probe/gen_drm_tsv_cov.py, not from a build.        **
;*******************************************************************************************************************************
